### Ran Playwright code
```js
await (async original=>{
const base='http://127.0.0.1:33590',O='/mnt/c/kidsmap/docs/qa/site-qa08-followup-fix-2026-10-18-pc-r1',rows=[],pageerrors=[],local_errors=[],console_errors=[],blocked=[];
const c=await original.context().browser().newContext({viewport:{width:1440,height:1000},serviceWorkers:'block'});await c.route('**/*',r=>new URL(r.request().url()).hostname==='127.0.0.1'?r.continue():(blocked.push(new URL(r.request().url()).hostname),r.abort()));const p=await c.newPage();p.on('pageerror',e=>pageerrors.push(String(e)));p.on('console',m=>{if(m.type()==='error')console_errors.push(m.text());});p.on('response',r=>{if(r.url().startsWith(base)&&r.status()>=400)local_errors.push({path:new URL(r.url()).pathname,status:r.status()});});await p.goto(base+'/qa/site08/login/super/');
const before=await(await p.request.get(base+'/qa/site08/all-state/')).json();
const measure=async()=>await p.evaluate(()=>{const visible=e=>e.getClientRects().length>0,rect=e=>{const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return{left:r.left,right:r.right,width:r.width,top:r.top,bottom:r.bottom}},main=rect(document.querySelector('.km-place-form-main')),cards=[...document.querySelectorAll('.km-place-form-main>.km-place-section')].filter(visible).map(rect),fields=[...document.querySelectorAll('#content-main input:not([type=hidden]),#content-main textarea,#content-main select,#content-main .file-upload,#content-main .select2-container,#content-main .help-block')].filter(visible).map(e=>({id:e.id,tag:e.tagName,...rect(e)})),action=document.querySelector('.km-place-sidebar-card--actions'),a=rect(action),overlap=(x,y)=>Math.min(x.right,y.right)>Math.max(x.left,y.left)+.75&&Math.min(x.bottom,y.bottom)>Math.max(x.top,y.top)+.75;return{document:document.documentElement.scrollWidth,main_width:main.width,root_width:rect(document.querySelector('#content-main')).width,visible_save_buttons:[...document.querySelectorAll('button[name=_save],button[name=_continue]')].filter(visible).length,field_widths:fields.map(f=>({id:f.id,width:f.width})),checks:{no_document_overflow:document.documentElement.scrollWidth<=innerWidth,fields_in_viewport:fields.every(f=>f.left>=-.75&&f.right<=innerWidth+.75),cards_within_main:cards.every(r=>r.left>=main.left-.75&&r.right<=main.right+.75),cards_not_covered_by_actions:!visible(action)||cards.every(r=>!overlap(r,a)),save_accessible:[...document.querySelectorAll('button[name=_save],button[name=_continue]')].filter(visible).length===2}};});
for(const lang of ['az','ru','en']){const csrf=(await c.cookies()).find(x=>x.name==='csrftoken').value;await p.request.post(base+'/i18n/setlang/',{headers:{'X-CSRFToken':csrf},form:{language:lang,next:'/admin/'},maxRedirects:0});
for(const model of ['sitebrandingsettings','siteaboutsettings','sitecontactssettings','sitefootersettings','siteemptystatesettings','sitevisibilitysettings','siteanalytics','catalogcontentsettings','sitegalleryimage'])for(const width of [360,390,768,1024,1280,1440]){
await p.setViewportSize({width,height:1000});const path='/admin/catalog/'+model+'/'+(model==='sitegalleryimage'?'add/':'1/change/');const response=await p.goto(base+path,{waitUntil:'load'});await p.waitForFunction(()=>!document.body.classList.contains('hold-transition'));await p.waitForTimeout(350);const baseline=await measure(),id=model+'-'+lang+'-'+width,screenshot=['sitebrandingsettings','sitefootersettings','catalogcontentsettings'].includes(model)&&[390,1440].includes(width);
if(screenshot)await p.screenshot({path:O+'/png/green-acceptance-'+id+'.png',fullPage:true,animations:'disabled'});
rows.push({id,model,lang,width,geometry:baseline,checks:{...baseline.checks,get200:response.status()===200,admin_language:await p.locator('html').getAttribute('lang')===lang}});
}}
const after=await(await p.request.get(base+'/qa/site08/all-state/')).json();await c.close();return{rows,pageerrors,local_errors,console_errors,blocked,no_fixture_write:JSON.stringify(before)===JSON.stringify(after),before,after,scope:'Prepared future actual-source baseline/acceptance; no saves; NOT RUN in PLAN. Actual-source acceptance; no injected classes/styles. NOT RUN in PLAN.'};
}
)(page);
```
